_G.ESPData = {
    espEnabled = false,
    espType = "Nome + Idade",
    selectedColor = "RGB",
    billboardGuis = {},
    highlights = {},
    lines = {},
    connections = {},
}
t1[20] = workspace.CurrentCamera
t1[21] = function()
    if _G.ESPData.selectedColor == "RGB" then
        local v145 = tick() % 5 / 5

        return Color3.fromHSV(v145, 1, 1)
    end

    local color3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    local color3_2 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    local color3_3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
    local color3_4 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
    local color3_5 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 170, 255)
    local color3_6 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
    local color3_7 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180)
    local color3_8 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 0, 128)
    local v154 = ({
        Branco = color3,
        Preto = color3_2,
        Vermelho = color3_3,
        Verde = color3_4,
        Azul = color3_5,
        Amarelo = color3_6,
        Rosa = color3_7,
        Roxo = color3_8,
    })[_G.ESPData.selectedColor]

    if not v154 then
        v154 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    end

    return v154
end
function _G.clearAllESP()

    for v157, v158 in pairs(_G.ESPData.billboardGuis) do

        local v159 = v158

        pcall(function()
            v159:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    for _, v in pairs(_G.ESPData.highlights) do
        local v162 = v

        pcall(function()
            v162:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    for v165, v166 in pairs(_G.ESPData.lines) do

        local v167 = v166

        pcall(function()
            v167:Remove()
        end)
    end
    for _, v in pairs(_G.ESPData.connections) do
        local v170 = v

        pcall(function()
            v170:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    _G.ESPData.billboardGuis = {}
    _G.ESPData.highlights = {}
    _G.ESPData.lines = {}
    _G.ESPData.connections = {}
end
local function v19(p8)
    if p8 == t1[1].LocalPlayer then
        return
    end

    if not p8.Character then
        return
    end

    if not p8.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
        return
    end

    local BillboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")

    BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50)
    BillboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
    BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
    BillboardGui.Parent = p8.Character.Head

    local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")

    TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
    TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    TextLabel.TextSize = 14

t1[22] = function(p9)

        return

t1[23] = function(p10)

        return

function _G.applyCurrentESP()
    _G.clearAllESP()

    if not _G.ESPData.espEnabled then
        return
    end

    for _, player in pairs(t1[1]:GetPlayers()) do
        if _G.ESPData.espType == "Nome + Idade" then
            v19(player)
        elseif _G.ESPData.espType == "Corpo (Highlight)" then
            t1[22](player)
        elseif _G.ESPData.espType == "Linhas" then
            t1[23](player)
        end
    end

    table.insert(
        _G.ESPData.connections,
        t1[3].RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if not _G.ESPData.espEnabled then
                return
            end

            local v800 = t1[21]()

            if _G.ESPData.espType == "Linhas" then
                for k, v in pairs(_G.ESPData.lines) do
                    local v803 = k

                    if v803.Character and v803.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        local v804, v805 =
                            t1[20]:WorldToViewportPoint(v803.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)

                        v.Visible = v805

                        if v805 then
                            v.From = Vector2.new(t1[20].ViewportSize.X / 2, t1[20].ViewportSize.Y)
                            v.To = Vector2.new(v804.X, v804.Y)
                            v.Color = v800
                        end
                    else
                        v.Visible = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    )
end

local function v21(p16, p17)
    local LocalPlayer = t1[1].LocalPlayer
    local v240 = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if not v240 then
        return
    end

    local AppliedDescription = v240:GetAppliedDescription()
    local ChangeCharacterBody = t1[24].ChangeCharacterBody
    local Torso = AppliedDescription.Torso
    local RightArm = AppliedDescription.RightArm
    local LeftArm = AppliedDescription.LeftArm
    local v246 = p16 or AppliedDescription.RightLeg

    if not p17 then
        p17 = AppliedDescription.LeftLeg
    end

    ChangeCharacterBody:InvokeServer({
        Torso,
        RightArm,
        LeftArm,
        v246,
        p17,
        0,
    })
end

                while t1[33] do

                    if Character then

                            end
                        end
                    end

                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
            end)
        end
    end,

t1[11]:AddButton({
    Name = "Remover Ban de Todas as Casas",
    Description = "Tenta remover o ban de todas as casas",
    Callback = function()
        local n1 = 0
        local n2 = 0
        for i = 1, 37 do
            local v273 = "BannedBlock" .. i
            local v274 = workspace:FindFirstChild(v273, true)

            if v274 then
                if pcall(function()
                    v274:Destroy()
                end) then
                    n1 += 1
                else
                    n2 += 1
                end
            end
        end
        for v277, v278 in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do

            local v279 = v278

            if v279.Name:match("BannedBlock") then
                if pcall(function()
                    v279:Destroy()
                end) then
                    n1 += 1
                else
                    n2 += 1
                end
            end
        end
        if n1 > 0 then
            local StarterGui = game.StarterGui
            local v281 = "Bans removidos de " .. n1 .. " casas!"

            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "Sucesso",
                Text = v281,
                Duration = 5,
            })
        end
        if n2 > 0 then
            local StarterGui = game.StarterGui
            local v283 = "Falha ao remover bans de " .. n2 .. " casas."

            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "unban Aviso",
                Text = v283,
                Duration = 5,
            })
        end
        if n1 == 0 and n2 == 0 then
            game.StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "Aviso",
                Text = "Nenhum ban encontrado para remover.",
                Duration = 5,
            })
        end
    end,
})

t1[65].AtualizarListaCarros = function(_)
    local t42 = {}
    local Vehicles = t1[62]:FindFirstChild("Vehicles")

    if Vehicles then
        local GetChildren = Vehicles.GetChildren

        for _, v in ipairs(GetChildren(Vehicles)) do
            if v.Name:match("Car$") then
                table.insert(t42, v.Name)
            end
        end
    end

    return t42
end
t1[65].GetVehicle = function(_, p54)
    local Vehicles = t1[62]:FindFirstChild("Vehicles")

    if not Vehicles then
        return
    end

    return Vehicles:FindFirstChild(p54)
end
t1[65].Sentar = function(_, p56)
    local Character = t1[63].Character

    if not Character then
        return false
    end

    local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if not Humanoid or not HumanoidRootPart then
        return false
    end

    Humanoid.Sit = false
    task.wait(0.1)
    HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p56.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
    task.wait(0.1)
    p56:Sit(Humanoid)
    task.wait(0.3)

    return p56 == Humanoid.SeatPart
end
t1[65].Sair = function(_, p58)
    local Character = t1[63].Character

    if not Character then
        return
    end

    local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

    if Humanoid and Humanoid.SeatPart then
        Humanoid.Sit = false
    end

    task.wait(0.1)

    if p58 then
        Character:PivotTo(CFrame.new(p58))
    end
end
t1[65].Trazer = function(_, p60, p61)
    if not p60 then
        return
    end

    if not p60.PrimaryPart then
        local v353 = p60:FindFirstChild("Body", true) or p60:FindFirstChild("Chassis", true)

        if not v353 then
            return
        end

        p60.PrimaryPart = v353
    end

    p60:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(p61 + Vector3.new(5, 0, 5)))
end

            local RightVector = HumanoidRootPart4.CFrame.RightVector

                + LookVector * math.random(-5, 8)

                Velocity = vector3_41,
            }

                local v905 = typeof(p78) == "Instance"

t1[13]:AddToggle({
    Name = " Loop Estourado",
    Description = "Som infinito forte",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(p151)
        t1[270] = p151

        if p151 then
            task.spawn(function()
                while t1[270] do
                    v106(t1[273])
                    task.wait(0.8)
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})
local function v107(p152)
    local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local t74 = {
        [1] = "ToolMusicText",
        [2] = p152,
    }

    ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("RE"):WaitForChild("PlayerToolEvent"):FireServer(unpack(t74))

    local t75 = {
        [1] = "PickHouseMusicText",
        [2] = p152,
    }
    local v695 = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("RE"):WaitForChild("1Player1sHous1e")
    local t76 = { unpack(t75) }

    v695:FireServer(v2(t76))

    local t77 = {
        [1] = "PickingCarMusicText",
        [2] = p152,
    }
    local v698 = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("RE"):WaitForChild("1Player1sCa1r")
    local t78 = { unpack(t77) }

    v698:FireServer(v2(t78))

    local t79 = {
        [1] = "PickingScooterMusicText",
        [2] = p152,
    }
    local v701 = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("RE"):WaitForChild("1NoMoto1rVehicle1s")
    local t80 = { unpack(t79) }

    v701:FireServer(v2(t80))
end
t1[14]:AddTextBox({
    Name = "ID da música",
    PlaceholderText = "Digite o ID e pressione Enter",
    Callback = function(p153)
        if p153 and p153 ~= "" then
            local _tostring = tostring

            v107(_tostring(p153))
        end
    end,
})
local function v108(p154, p155, p156)
    local t81 = {}
    local t82 = {}

    for k, v in pairs(p155) do
        for _, v8 in ipairs(v) do
            if v8.name ~= "" then
                table.insert(t81, v8.name)
                t82[v8.name] = {
                    id = v8.id,
                    category = k,
                }
            end
        end
    end

    t1[14]:AddDropdown({
        Name = p154,
        Description = "all",
        Default = p156,
        Multi = false,
        Options = t81,
        Callback = function(p157)
            if p157 and t82[p157] then
                local id = t82[p157].id

                if id and (id ~= "" and id ~= "4354908569") then
                    v107((tostring(id)))
                end
            end
        end,
    })
end

            local v1240 = t1[278]
            local v1241 = tick() * v1240 % 10 / 10

            local PlayerGui = t1[274]:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")

                if PropsColor and PropsColor:FindFirstChild("SetColor") then
                    pcall(function()
                        PropsColor.SetColor:FireServer(color3)
                    end)
                end
            end

            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
end
